import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:ui';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import '../theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../models/core_types.dart';
import '../main.dart';

enum ExamPhase { lobby, active, result }

class _DataPanel extends StatelessWidget {
  final Widget child;
  final EdgeInsetsGeometry padding;
  final Color borderColor;
  const _DataPanel({required this.child, this.padding = const EdgeInsets.all(16), this.borderColor = Colors.white12});
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ClipRRect(
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
      child: BackdropFilter(
        filter: ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 8.0, sigmaY: 8.0),
        child: Container(
          padding: padding,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: AppColors.slate900.withOpacity(0.8),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            border: Border.all(color: borderColor, width: 1.5),
          ),
          child: child,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _SolidButton extends StatelessWidget {
  final String text; final Color color; final VoidCallback onPressed; final IconData? icon;
  const _SolidButton({required this.text, required this.color, required this.onPressed, this.icon});
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ElevatedButton(
      onPressed: () { HapticFeedback.mediumImpact(); onPressed(); },
      style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: color, foregroundColor: AppColors.slate950, elevation: 0, shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)), padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16)),
      child: Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center, children: [if (icon != null) ...[Icon(icon, size: 20), const SizedBox(width: 8)], Text(text, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, letterSpacing: 0.5))]),
    );
  }
}

class _DevOpsFourKeysDashboard extends StatelessWidget {
  final double failureRate; final int avgSecondsPerQuestion;
  const _DevOpsFourKeysDashboard({required this.failureRate, required this.avgSecondsPerQuestion});
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return _DataPanel(
      borderColor: AppColors.blue500.withOpacity(0.5),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween, children: const [Row(children: [Icon(Icons.speed_rounded, color: AppColors.blue500, size: 20), SizedBox(width: 8), Text('DevOps DORA 四大作戰指標', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate50, fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))]), Text('DORA Metrics', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.blue500, fontSize: 10, fontFamily: 'monospace', fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))]),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          Row(children: [Expanded(child: _buildMetricCard('變更失敗率 (錯題率)', '${failureRate.toStringAsFixed(1)}%', failureRate < 25 ? AppColors.emerald400 : AppColors.rose500)), const SizedBox(width: 10), Expanded(child: _buildMetricCard('平均前置時間 (解題)', '${avgSecondsPerQuestion}s / 題', avgSecondsPerQuestion < 45 ? AppColors.emerald400 : AppColors.amber400))]),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
  Widget _buildMetricCard(String title, String value, Color color) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.black45, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8), border: Border.all(color: color.withOpacity(0.3))),
      child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [Text(title, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)), const SizedBox(height: 6), Text(value, style: TextStyle(color: color, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontFamily: 'monospace'))]),
    );
  }
}

class MockExamArenaView extends StatefulWidget {
  const MockExamArenaView({super.key});
  @override
  State<MockExamArenaView> createState() => _MockExamArenaViewState();
}

class _MockExamArenaViewState extends State<MockExamArenaView> {
  ExamPhase _phase = ExamPhase.lobby;
  List<QuestionItem> _examQuestions = [];
  final Map<int, List<int>> _answers = {};
  final Set<int> _flaggedIndices = {};
  Timer? _timer;
  int _secondsRemaining = 6000;
  int _currentIndex = 0;
  int _singleScore = 0;
  int _multiScore = 0;
  int _totalScore = 0;
  List<QuestionItem> _wrongQuestions = [];

  @override
  void dispose() { _timer?.cancel(); super.dispose(); }

  void _startExam() {
    HapticFeedback.heavyImpact();
    final all = globalMasterState.allQuestions;
    final singles = all.where((q) => q.type == 'single').toList()..shuffle();
    final multiples = all.where((q) => q.type == 'multiple').toList()..shuffle();
    final paper = <QuestionItem>[];
    paper.addAll(singles.take(60)); paper.addAll(multiples.take(20));
    setState(() { _examQuestions = paper; _answers.clear(); _flaggedIndices.clear(); _secondsRemaining = 6000; _currentIndex = 0; _phase = ExamPhase.active; _wrongQuestions.clear(); });
    _timer?.cancel();
    _timer = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 1), (timer) {
      if (!mounted) { timer.cancel(); return; }
      if (_secondsRemaining > 0) setState(() => _secondsRemaining--); else _finishExam(isTimeOut: true);
    });
  }

  void _finishExam({bool isTimeOut = false}) {
    _timer?.cancel(); HapticFeedback.heavyImpact();
    int singlePts = 0; int multiPts = 0; final wrongs = <QuestionItem>[];
    for (int i = 0; i < _examQuestions.length; i++) {
      final q = _examQuestions[i]; final userSelected = _answers[i] ?? []; final officialAns = q.answer;
      bool passed = false;
      if (q.type == 'single') { if (userSelected.isNotEmpty && officialAns.contains(userSelected.first)) { passed = true; singlePts += 1; } }
      else { final uSet = userSelected.toSet(); final oSet = officialAns.toSet(); if (uSet.length == oSet.length && uSet.containsAll(oSet)) { passed = true; multiPts += 2; } }
      if (!passed) wrongs.add(q);
    }
    setState(() { _singleScore = singlePts; _multiScore = multiPts; _totalScore = singlePts + multiPts; _wrongQuestions = wrongs; _phase = ExamPhase.result; });
    if (isTimeOut && mounted) ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('⏰ 時間已到！系統已自動封存閱卷。'), backgroundColor: AppColors.rose500));
  }

  void _onOptionTapped(int optionIndex) {
    HapticFeedback.selectionClick();
    final q = _examQuestions[_currentIndex]; final optionNumber = optionIndex + 1; final currentSelected = List<int>.from(_answers[_currentIndex] ?? []);
    if (q.type == 'single') {
      _answers[_currentIndex] = [optionNumber];
      if (_currentIndex < _examQuestions.length - 1) { Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 300), () { if (mounted && _phase == ExamPhase.active) setState(() => _currentIndex++); }); }
    } else {
      if (currentSelected.contains(optionNumber)) currentSelected.remove(optionNumber); else currentSelected.add(optionNumber);
      if (currentSelected.isEmpty) _answers.remove(_currentIndex); else _answers[_currentIndex] = currentSelected;
    }
    setState(() {});
  }

  void _showSubmitConfirmationDialog() {
    HapticFeedback.mediumImpact();
    final unanswered = _examQuestions.length - _answers.keys.length;
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) {
        return AlertDialog(
          backgroundColor: AppColors.slate900,
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12), side: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.amber400, width: 2)),
          title: const Text('⚠️ 確認封存交卷？', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate50, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
          content: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [Text('剩餘時間：${_formatTime(_secondsRemaining)}', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.amber400, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16)), const SizedBox(height: 12), Text(unanswered > 0 ? '警告：您還有 $unanswered 題尚未填答！' : '系統檢測：80 題已全數填答完畢。', style: TextStyle(color: unanswered > 0 ? AppColors.rose500 : AppColors.emerald400, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)), const SizedBox(height: 8), const Text('交卷後將無法修改答案，並立即進入資料分析程序。', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400, fontSize: 12))]),
          actions: [TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context), child: const Text('返回作答', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400))), ElevatedButton(onPressed: () { Navigator.pop(context); _finishExam(); }, style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: AppColors.amber400, foregroundColor: AppColors.slate950), child: const Text('確認交卷', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)))],
        );
      },
    );
  }

  void _showAnswerSheetModal() {
    HapticFeedback.lightImpact();
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context, isScrollControlled: true, backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      builder: (context) {
        return DraggableScrollableSheet(
          initialChildSize: 0.85, minChildSize: 0.5, maxChildSize: 0.95, expand: false,
          builder: (context, scrollCtrl) {
            return _DataPanel(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
              child: Column(
                children: [
                  Container(width: 40, height: 5, margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8), decoration: BoxDecoration(color: AppColors.slate700, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4))),
                  Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween, children: [const Text('📊 全景狀態矩陣 (答題卡)', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: AppColors.slate50)), Text('完成度: ${_answers.length}/${_examQuestions.length}', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.emerald400, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontFamily: 'monospace'))]),
                  const Divider(color: AppColors.slate800, height: 24),
                  Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceEvenly, children: [_buildLegendDot(AppColors.emerald400, '已答'), _buildLegendDot(AppColors.rose500, '標記疑問'), _buildLegendDot(AppColors.slate800, '未作答')]),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  Expanded(
                    child: GridView.builder(
                      controller: scrollCtrl, physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(), gridDelegate: const SliverGridDelegateWithFixedCrossAxisCount(crossAxisCount: 7, crossAxisSpacing: 8, mainAxisSpacing: 8),
                      itemCount: _examQuestions.length,
                      itemBuilder: (context, i) {
                        final isAnswered = _answers.containsKey(i); final isFlagged = _flaggedIndices.contains(i); final isCurrent = i == _currentIndex;
                        Color tileBg = AppColors.slate800; Color textCol = AppColors.slate400; Color borderCol = Colors.transparent;
                        if (isFlagged) { tileBg = AppColors.rose500.withOpacity(0.2); textCol = AppColors.rose500; borderCol = AppColors.rose500; }
                        else if (isAnswered) { tileBg = AppColors.emerald400.withOpacity(0.2); textCol = AppColors.emerald400; }
                        if (isCurrent) borderCol = AppColors.amber400;
                        return GestureDetector(onTap: () { HapticFeedback.selectionClick(); setState(() => _currentIndex = i); Navigator.pop(context); }, child: Container(decoration: BoxDecoration(color: tileBg, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6), border: Border.all(color: borderCol, width: isCurrent ? 2.5 : 1)), alignment: Alignment.center, child: Text('${i + 1}', style: TextStyle(color: textCol, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 13))));
                      },
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            );
          },
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildLegendDot(Color color, String label) {
    return Row(children: [Container(width: 10, height: 10, decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color, shape: BoxShape.circle)), const SizedBox(width: 4), Text(label, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400, fontSize: 12))]);
  }
  String _formatTime(int totalSeconds) { final m = (totalSeconds ~/ 60).toString().padLeft(2, '0'); final s = (totalSeconds % 60).toString().padLeft(2, '0'); return '$m:$s'; }

  Widget _buildActiveExamView() {

  Widget _buildResultView() {
    final isPassed = _totalScore >= 60;
    final failureRate = (_wrongQuestions.length / 80) * 100;
    final elapsedSeconds = 6000 - _secondsRemaining;
    final avgSecPerQ = elapsedSeconds ~/ 80;

    return SingleChildScrollView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20.0),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          _DataPanel(
            borderColor: isPassed ? AppColors.emerald400 : AppColors.rose500, padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
            child: Column(
              children: [
                Icon(isPassed ? Icons.verified_rounded : Icons.dangerous_rounded, size: 64, color: isPassed ? AppColors.emerald400 : AppColors.rose500), const SizedBox(height: 12), Text(isPassed ? '判定結果：合格' : '判定結果：不合格', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: isPassed ? AppColors.emerald400 : AppColors.rose500)), const SizedBox(height: 16), Text('$_totalScore', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 64, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: isPassed ? AppColors.emerald400 : AppColors.rose500, fontFamily: 'monospace')), const Text('最終測驗總分 (滿分 100)', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400, fontSize: 13)), const Divider(color: AppColors.slate800, height: 32),
                Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceAround, // ✅ 已修復 MainAxisAlignment 拼寫錯誤
                  children: [
                    Column(children: [Text('$_singleScore / 60', style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: AppColors.slate50)), const SizedBox(height: 4), const Text('單選題得分', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400, fontSize: 12))]),
                    Container(height: 40, width: 1, color: AppColors.slate700),
                    Column(children: [Text('$_multiScore / 40', style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: AppColors.slate50)), const SizedBox(height: 4), const Text('複選題得分', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400, fontSize: 12))]),
                  ],
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 20),
          _DevOpsFourKeysDashboard(failureRate: failureRate, avgSecondsPerQuestion: avgSecPerQ),
          const SizedBox(height: 20),
          if (_wrongQuestions.isNotEmpty) ...[
            _DataPanel(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Row(children: const [Icon(Icons.analytics_rounded, color: AppColors.amber400, size: 20), SizedBox(width: 8), Text('CIO 戰損資料復盤', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate50, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))]), const SizedBox(height: 12), Text('本次測驗共產生 ${_wrongQuestions.length} 道錯誤判斷 (包含未答或未完全答對之複選題)。', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400, fontSize: 13, height: 1.5)), const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  _SolidButton(
                    text: '封裝錯題並寫入 FSRS 資料庫', color: AppColors.amber400, icon: Icons.memory_rounded,
                    onPressed: () {
                      for (final q in _wrongQuestions) globalMasterState.recordWrongAnswer(q.id);
                      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('✅ 成功將 ${_wrongQuestions.length} 題寫入急診佇列！'), backgroundColor: AppColors.emerald400));
                      setState(() { _wrongQuestions.clear(); });
                    },
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
          ],
          OutlinedButton.icon(
            onPressed: () { HapticFeedback.selectionClick(); setState(() => _phase = ExamPhase.lobby); },
            icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh_rounded, color: AppColors.slate50), label: const Text('返回大廳重啟測驗', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate50)),
            style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(side: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.slate700), padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppColors.slate950,
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('🏛️ 全真模擬考場', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900)), elevation: 0),
      body: SafeArea(child: _phase == ExamPhase.active ? _buildActiveExamView() : (_phase == ExamPhase.result ? _buildResultView() : _buildLobbyView())),
    );
  }
}

// ==========================================
// 專案名稱：電光證照霸 PRO (ElectroSolar Pass)
// 檔案：lib/screens/paywall_screen.dart
// 說明：[CQO 跨平台相容版] 加入 kIsWeb 平台隔離，防止 Chrome 崩潰
// ==========================================

import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:ui';

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ClipRRect(
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
      child: BackdropFilter(
        filter: ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 20.0, sigmaY: 20.0),
        child: Container(

          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: AppColors.slate900.withOpacity(0.75),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),

          ),

        ),
      ),

}

class _AppleStylePayButton extends StatefulWidget {
  final String label;
  final String priceText;
  final VoidCallback? onPressed;
  final bool isLoading;

  const _AppleStylePayButton({required this.label, required this.priceText, required this.onPressed, this.isLoading = false});

  @override
  State<_AppleStylePayButton> createState() => _AppleStylePayButtonState();
}

class _AppleStylePayButtonState extends State<_AppleStylePayButton> {
  bool _isPressed = false;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final bool isDisabled = widget.isLoading || widget.onPressed == null;
    return GestureDetector(
      onTapDown: (_) { if (!isDisabled) { HapticFeedback.lightImpact(); setState(() => _isPressed = true); } },
      onTapUp: (_) { if (!isDisabled) { HapticFeedback.heavyImpact(); setState(() => _isPressed = false); widget.onPressed!(); } },
      onTapCancel: () => setState(() => _isPressed = false),
      child: AnimatedContainer(
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 120),
        curve: Curves.easeOut,
        height: 56,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: _isPressed || isDisabled ? AppColors.amber400.withOpacity(0.5) : AppColors.amber400,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
          boxShadow: [BoxShadow(color: AppColors.amber400.withOpacity(_isPressed || isDisabled ? 0.0 : 0.4), blurRadius: _isPressed ? 4 : 12, offset: Offset(0, _isPressed ? 2 : 4))],
        ),
        child: Row(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
          children: [
            Row(
              children: [
                const Icon(Icons.apple_rounded, color: AppColors.slate950, size: 22),
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                Text(widget.isLoading ? '安全連線中...' : widget.label, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate950, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900)),
              ],
            ),
            if (widget.isLoading)
              const SizedBox(width: 20, height: 20, child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: AppColors.slate950, strokeWidth: 2))
            else
              Text(widget.priceText, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate950, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontFamily: 'monospace')),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

final List<String> _kProductIds = ['esp_pro_30d_290', 'esp_pro_180d_390', 'esp_pro_lifetime_590'];

class HardcorePaywallView extends StatefulWidget {
  const HardcorePaywallView({super.key});
  @override
  State<HardcorePaywallView> createState() => _HardcorePaywallViewState();
}

class _HardcorePaywallViewState extends State<HardcorePaywallView> {
  final TextEditingController _licenseKeyCtrl = TextEditingController();
  String _activationFeedback = '';
  int _selectedTierIndex = 2;

  InAppPurchase? _iap;
  StreamSubscription<List<PurchaseDetails>>? _subscription;
  List<ProductDetails> _products = [];
  bool _isIapAvailable = false;
  bool _isPurchasePending = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    // 【核心防護】：Web 瀏覽器環境下不初始化原生 IAP，防止 UnimplementedError 崩潰
    if (!kIsWeb) {
      _iap = InAppPurchase.instance;
      _initStoreInfo();
    }
  }

  Future<void> _initStoreInfo() async {
    if (_iap == null) return;
    final Stream<List<PurchaseDetails>> purchaseUpdated = _iap!.purchaseStream;
    _subscription = purchaseUpdated.listen((purchaseDetailsList) {
      _listenToPurchaseUpdated(purchaseDetailsList);
    }, onDone: () => _subscription?.cancel(), onError: (error) => debugPrint('IAP Error: $error'));

    final bool isAvailable = await _iap!.isAvailable();
    if (!isAvailable) {
      if (mounted) setState(() { _isIapAvailable = false; _products = []; });
      return;
    }

    final ProductDetailsResponse response = await _iap!.queryProductDetails(_kProductIds.toSet());
    if (response.error == null && response.productDetails.isNotEmpty) {
      final fetchedProducts = response.productDetails;
      fetchedProducts.sort((a, b) => a.rawPrice.compareTo(b.rawPrice));
      if (mounted) setState(() { _isIapAvailable = true; _products = fetchedProducts; });
    }
  }

  void _listenToPurchaseUpdated(List<PurchaseDetails> purchaseDetailsList) {
    for (final PurchaseDetails purchaseDetails in purchaseDetailsList) {
      if (purchaseDetails.status == PurchaseStatus.pending) {
        if (mounted) setState(() => _isPurchasePending = true);
      } else {
        if (mounted) setState(() => _isPurchasePending = false);
        if (purchaseDetails.status == PurchaseStatus.error) {
          ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('❌ 交易失敗：${purchaseDetails.error?.message}'), backgroundColor: AppColors.rose500));
        } else if (purchaseDetails.status == PurchaseStatus.purchased || purchaseDetails.status == PurchaseStatus.restored) {
          globalMasterState.activateLicense('VIP888');
          ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('💳 Apple Pay 授權成功！PRO 權限已寫入！'), backgroundColor: AppColors.emerald400));
          if (Navigator.canPop(context)) Navigator.pop(context);
        }
        if (purchaseDetails.pendingCompletePurchase) {
          _iap?.completePurchase(purchaseDetails);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _subscription?.cancel();
    _licenseKeyCtrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _attemptUnlock({String? directCode}) {
    final code = directCode ?? _licenseKeyCtrl.text;
    final success = globalMasterState.activateLicense(code);
    if (success) {
      HapticFeedback.heavyImpact();
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('🎉 恭喜！尊榮 PRO 旗艦版已全功能永久啟用！'), backgroundColor: AppColors.emerald400));
    } else {
      HapticFeedback.vibrate();
      setState(() => _activationFeedback = '❌ 授權碼無效，請輸入 VIP888 或 PASS2026');
    }
  }

  void _buyProduct() {
    if (kIsWeb || _iap == null || !_isIapAvailable || _products.isEmpty) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('💡 Web 環境為展示模式，請使用序號 VIP888 解鎖。真機將呼叫 Apple Pay！'), backgroundColor: AppColors.amber400));
      return;
    }
    final targetId = _kProductIds[_selectedTierIndex];
    final product = _products.firstWhere((p) => p.id == targetId, orElse: () => _products.last);
    _iap!.buyNonConsumable(purchaseParam: PurchaseParam(productDetails: product));
  }

  void _openInstantCheckoutModal() {
    HapticFeedback.mediumImpact();
    final tierNames = ['考前 30 天急救包', '單一證照 180 天穩過包', '雙證照終身買斷旗艦包'];
    final originalPrices = ['NT\$ 490', 'NT\$ 690', 'NT\$ 890'];
    final promoPrices = ['NT\$ 290', 'NT\$ 390', 'NT\$ 590'];

    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context, isScrollControlled: true, backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      builder: (context) {
        return _IosGlassCard(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
            children: [
              Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                children: [
                  const Text('⚡ Apple IAP 快速結帳通道', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: AppColors.amber400)),
                  IconButton(icon: const Icon(Icons.close, color: AppColors.slate400, size: 20), onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context)),
                ],
              ),
              const Divider(color: Colors.white24, height: 20),
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16), decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.black45, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                child: Column(
                  children: [
                    Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween, children: [Text(tierNames[_selectedTierIndex], style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate50, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)), Text(originalPrices[_selectedTierIndex], style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400, decoration: TextDecoration.lineThrough, fontSize: 13))]),
                    const SizedBox(height: 6),
                    Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween, children: const [Text('考前衝刺限時折扣', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.rose500, fontSize: 12)), Text('- NT\$ 300', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.rose500, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))]),
                    const Divider(color: Colors.white12, height: 16),
                    Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween, children: [const Text('應付總額 (含稅)', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate50, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)), Text(promoPrices[_selectedTierIndex], style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.emerald400, fontSize: 22, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontFamily: 'monospace'))]),

class _GlassCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final Widget child;
  final EdgeInsetsGeometry padding;

  const _GlassCard({required this.child, this.padding = const EdgeInsets.all(16)});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ClipRRect(
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
      child: BackdropFilter(
        filter: ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 16.0, sigmaY: 16.0),
        child: Container(
          padding: padding,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: AppColors.slate900.withOpacity(0.75),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
            border: Border.all(color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.15), width: 1),
          ),
          child: child,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _DuoChunkyButton extends StatefulWidget {
  final String text;
  final Color color;
  final Color shadowColor;
  final VoidCallback? onPressed;
  final double height;

  const _DuoChunkyButton({
    required this.text, required this.color, required this.shadowColor, required this.onPressed, this.height = 56.0,
  });

  @override
  State<_DuoChunkyButton> createState() => _DuoChunkyButtonState();
}

class _DuoChunkyButtonState extends State<_DuoChunkyButton> {
  bool _isPressed = false;
  void _onTapDown(TapDownDetails details) { if (widget.onPressed == null) return; HapticFeedback.lightImpact(); setState(() => _isPressed = true); }
  void _onTapUp(TapUpDetails details) { if (widget.onPressed == null) return; setState(() => _isPressed = false); widget.onPressed!(); }
  void _onTapCancel() => setState(() => _isPressed = false);

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final bool isDisabled = widget.onPressed == null;
    final double pushOffset = _isPressed ? 6.0 : 0.0;
    final bgColor = isDisabled ? AppColors.slate800 : widget.color;
    final sColor = isDisabled ? AppColors.slate900 : widget.shadowColor;
    final fgColor = isDisabled ? AppColors.slate500 : AppColors.slate950;

    return GestureDetector(
      onTapDown: _onTapDown, onTapUp: _onTapUp, onTapCancel: _onTapCancel,
      child: AnimatedContainer(
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 100), curve: Curves.easeOut, height: widget.height,
        margin: EdgeInsets.only(top: pushOffset, bottom: 6.0 - pushOffset),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(color: bgColor, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16), boxShadow: [BoxShadow(color: sColor, offset: Offset(0, 6.0 - pushOffset))]),
        alignment: Alignment.center,
        child: Text(widget.text, style: TextStyle(color: fgColor, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, letterSpacing: 1.0)),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// Atish 星系圖渲染器
class _ConstellationGraphPainter extends CustomPainter {
  final int nodeCount; final double animationValue;
  _ConstellationGraphPainter({required this.nodeCount, required this.animationValue});
  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    final center = Offset(size.width / 2, size.height / 2);
    final radius = math.min(size.width, size.height) * 0.38;
    canvas.drawCircle(center, 24 * animationValue, Paint()..color = AppColors.rose500.withOpacity(0.2 * animationValue)..maskFilter = const MaskFilter.blur(BlurStyle.normal, 16));
    canvas.drawCircle(center, 12, Paint()..color = AppColors.rose500..style = PaintingStyle.fill);
    final linePaint = Paint()..color = AppColors.amber400.withOpacity(0.5)..strokeWidth = 1.5..style = PaintingStyle.stroke;
    final nodePaint = Paint()..color = AppColors.emerald400..style = PaintingStyle.fill;
    for (int i = 0; i < nodeCount; i++) {
      final angle = (i * 2 * math.pi / nodeCount) - (math.pi / 2) + (animationValue * 0.1);
      final targetNode = center + Offset(radius * math.cos(angle), radius * math.sin(angle));
      canvas.drawLine(center, targetNode, linePaint);
      final textPainter = TextPainter(text: TextSpan(text: ' ${(85 - i * 4)}%', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.amber400, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontFamily: 'monospace')), textDirection: TextDirection.ltr)..layout();
      textPainter.paint(canvas, Offset((center.dx + targetNode.dx) / 2, (center.dy + targetNode.dy) / 2) - Offset(textPainter.width / 2, textPainter.height + 4));
      canvas.drawCircle(targetNode, 8 * animationValue, nodePaint);
    }
  }
  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(covariant _ConstellationGraphPainter oldDelegate) => oldDelegate.animationValue != animationValue || oldDelegate.nodeCount != nodeCount;
}

/// Kevin 蘇格拉底遮罩
class _SocraticMaskOverlay extends StatelessWidget {
  final String questionText; final VoidCallback onShatter;
  const _SocraticMaskOverlay({required this.questionText, required this.onShatter});
  String _prompt(String q) {
    if (q.contains('電壓降')) return '大師點撥：計算壓降前，請先回憶這是單相還是三相？係數是多少？';
    if (q.contains('距離')) return '大師點撥：法規數字題最易混淆。請閉上眼睛，這題是「以上」還是「以下」？';
    return '大師點撥：看解析前，請先在腦中重播一次這題的關鍵字，想好了嗎？';
  }
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ClipRRect(
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
      child: BackdropFilter(
        filter: ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 8.0, sigmaY: 8.0),
        child: Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(color: AppColors.slate950.withOpacity(0.85), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16), border: Border.all(color: AppColors.blue500.withOpacity(0.5), width: 1.5)),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              const Icon(Icons.psychology_alt_rounded, color: AppColors.blue500, size: 48),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              const Text('主動提取訓練', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.blue500, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, letterSpacing: 2.0)),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              Text(_prompt(questionText), textAlign: TextAlign.center, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate50, fontSize: 14, height: 1.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
              const SizedBox(height: 24),
              _DuoChunkyButton(text: '💡 想清楚了，擊碎遮罩看解析', color: AppColors.blue500, shadowColor: const Color(0xFF1E3A8A), height: 48, onPressed: () { HapticFeedback.heavyImpact(); onShatter(); }),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// Vittorio 自適應折疊器
class _AdaptiveExplanationFolder extends StatefulWidget {
  final double memoryStability; final Widget compactChild; final Widget fullChild;
  const _AdaptiveExplanationFolder({required this.memoryStability, required this.compactChild, required this.fullChild});
  @override
  State<_AdaptiveExplanationFolder> createState() => _AdaptiveExplanationFolderState();
}
class _AdaptiveExplanationFolderState extends State<_AdaptiveExplanationFolder> {
  late bool _isExpanded;
  @override
  void initState() { super.initState(); _isExpanded = widget.memoryStability < 3.0; }
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isExpert = widget.memoryStability >= 3.0;
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
      children: [
        GestureDetector(
          onTap: () { HapticFeedback.selectionClick(); setState(() => _isExpanded = !_isExpanded); },
          child: Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 8),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.06), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10), border: Border.all(color: Colors.white12)),
            child: Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
              children: [
                Row(children: [Icon(isExpert ? Icons.workspace_premium_rounded : Icons.school_rounded, color: isExpert ? AppColors.amber400 : AppColors.blue500, size: 16), const SizedBox(width: 6), Text(isExpert ? 'iOS 老手狀態 (已精熟 · 預設摺疊詳解)' : 'iOS 新手狀態 (載入完整推導鷹架)', style: TextStyle(color: isExpert ? AppColors.amber400 : AppColors.blue500, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))]),
                Row(children: [Text(_isExpanded ? '收起' : '展開完整推導', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400, fontSize: 11)), Icon(_isExpanded ? Icons.keyboard_arrow_up_rounded : Icons.keyboard_arrow_down_rounded, color: AppColors.slate400, size: 18)])
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        AnimatedCrossFade(firstChild: widget.compactChild, secondChild: widget.fullChild, crossFadeState: _isExpanded ? CrossFadeState.showSecond : CrossFadeState.showFirst, duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300), firstCurve: Curves.easeOutCirc, secondCurve: Curves.easeOutCirc),
      ],
    );
  }
}

/// Susan 雷射掃描防護罩
class _AerospaceGroundingScan extends StatefulWidget {
  final Widget child;
  const _AerospaceGroundingScan({required this.child});
  @override
  State<_AerospaceGroundingScan> createState() => _AerospaceGroundingScanState();
}
class _AerospaceGroundingScanState extends State<_AerospaceGroundingScan> with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late AnimationController _scanController;
  @override
  void initState() { super.initState(); _scanController = AnimationController(vsync: this, duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 1400))..forward(); }
  @override
  void dispose() { _scanController.dispose(); super.dispose(); }
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Stack(
      children: [
        widget.child,
        AnimatedBuilder(
          animation: _scanController,
          builder: (context, child) {
            if (_scanController.isCompleted) return const SizedBox.shrink();
            return Positioned.fill(child: ClipRRect(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16), child: CustomPaint(painter: _LaserScanPainter(progress: _scanController.value))));
          },
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}
class _LaserScanPainter extends CustomPainter {
  final double progress;
  _LaserScanPainter({required this.progress});
  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    final y = size.height * progress;
    canvas.drawLine(Offset(0, y), Offset(size.width, y), Paint()..color = AppColors.emerald400.withOpacity(0.4)..strokeWidth = 12.0..maskFilter = const MaskFilter.blur(BlurStyle.normal, 8));
    canvas.drawLine(Offset(0, y), Offset(size.width, y), Paint()..color = AppColors.emerald400..strokeWidth = 2.5..style = PaintingStyle.stroke);
  }
  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(covariant _LaserScanPainter oldDelegate) => oldDelegate.progress != progress;
}

/// Mark 微觀決策甘特圖
class _MicroDecisionGantt extends StatelessWidget {
  final int readingSeconds; final int hesitationSeconds; final int decisionSeconds;
  const _MicroDecisionGantt({required this.readingSeconds, required this.hesitationSeconds, required this.decisionSeconds});
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final total = math.max(1, readingSeconds + hesitationSeconds + decisionSeconds);
    final readingRatio = readingSeconds / total; final hesitationRatio = hesitationSeconds / total; final decisionRatio = decisionSeconds / total;
    final isHesitant = hesitationSeconds > 5;
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 10, bottom: 4), padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.04), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10), border: Border.all(color: isHesitant ? AppColors.rose500.withOpacity(0.4) : Colors.white12)),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Row(children: const [Icon(Icons.timeline_rounded, color: AppColors.blue500, size: 15), SizedBox(width: 6), Text('Jira 微觀決策分析', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.blue500, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))]),
              Text(isHesitant ? '⚠️ 猶豫 $hesitationSeconds 秒 (建議強化熟練度)' : '⚡ 決策果斷 (直覺記憶)', style: TextStyle(color: isHesitant ? AppColors.rose500 : AppColors.emerald400, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          ClipRRect(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4), child: SizedBox(height: 6, child: Row(children: [if (readingRatio > 0) Expanded(flex: (readingRatio * 100).round(), child: Container(color: AppColors.blue500)), if (hesitationRatio > 0) Expanded(flex: (hesitationRatio * 100).round(), child: Container(color: AppColors.rose500)), if (decisionRatio > 0) Expanded(flex: (decisionRatio * 100).round(), child: Container(color: AppColors.emerald400))]))),
          const SizedBox(height: 6),
          Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween, children: [Text('讀題 ${readingSeconds}s', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400, fontSize: 10)), Text('猶豫 ${hesitationSeconds}s', style: TextStyle(color: isHesitant ? AppColors.rose500 : AppColors.slate400, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: isHesitant ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.normal)), Text('決策 ${decisionSeconds}s', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400, fontSize: 10))]),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// Werner 神經突觸儀表
class _SynapseReplayIndicator extends StatelessWidget {
  final double currentStability;
  const _SynapseReplayIndicator({required this.currentStability});
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final projectedStability = (currentStability == 0) ? 1.0 : currentStability * 2.5;
    final projectedNextDate = DateTime.now().add(Duration(days: projectedStability.round()));
    final dateStr = "${projectedNextDate.year}/${projectedNextDate.month}/${projectedNextDate.day}";
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8, bottom: 4), padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 8),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(color: AppColors.emerald400.withOpacity(0.12), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10), border: Border.all(color: AppColors.emerald400.withOpacity(0.4))),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          Row(children: const [Icon(Icons.psychology_rounded, color: AppColors.emerald400, size: 16), SizedBox(width: 6), Text('AWS FSRS 記憶狀態機', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.emerald400, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))]),
          Text('穩定度 ${currentStability.toStringAsFixed(1)}d ➔ 評級推遲至 $dateStr', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.emerald400, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontFamily: 'monospace')),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ==========================================
// 考點雷達動態視圖 (Meta Knowledge Graph)
// ==========================================

class _KnowledgeGraphRadar extends StatefulWidget {
  final QuestionItem currentQuestion;
  final List<QuestionItem> similarQuestions;
  final Function(String) onNodeTapped;

  const _KnowledgeGraphRadar({
    required this.currentQuestion,
    required this.similarQuestions,
    required this.onNodeTapped,
  });

  @override
  State<_KnowledgeGraphRadar> createState() => _KnowledgeGraphRadarState();
}

class _KnowledgeGraphRadarState extends State<_KnowledgeGraphRadar> with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late AnimationController _pulseController;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _pulseController = AnimationController(
      vsync: this,
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 2000),
    )..repeat(reverse: true);
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _pulseController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (widget.similarQuestions.isEmpty) return const SizedBox.shrink();

    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
      children: [
        const SizedBox(height: 24),
        const Divider(color: Colors.white24, height: 1),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        Row(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
          children: const [
            Text('🎯 考點星系圖・深層結構雷達', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: AppColors.amber400)),
            Text('Meta Graph Engine', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 10, color: AppColors.slate400, fontFamily: 'monospace')),
          ],
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        Container(
          height: 220,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.3),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            border: Border.all(color: AppColors.slate800),
          ),
          child: AnimatedBuilder(
            animation: _pulseController,
            builder: (context, child) {
              return Stack(
                children: [
                  Positioned.fill(
                    child: CustomPaint(
                      painter: _ConstellationGraphPainter(
                        nodeCount: widget.similarQuestions.length,
                        animationValue: _pulseController.value,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  ...List.generate(widget.similarQuestions.length, (i) {
                    final simQ = widget.similarQuestions[i];
                    final angle = (i * 2 * math.pi / widget.similarQuestions.length) - (math.pi / 2) + (_pulseController.value * 0.1);
                    return Align(
                      alignment: Alignment(math.cos(angle) * 0.8, math.sin(angle) * 0.8),
                      child: GestureDetector(
                        onTap: () => widget.onNodeTapped(simQ.id),
                        child: Container(
                          width: 100,
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(6),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: AppColors.slate900.withOpacity(0.9),
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                            border: Border.all(color: AppColors.emerald400.withOpacity(0.5)),
                            boxShadow: [BoxShadow(color: AppColors.emerald400.withOpacity(0.2), blurRadius: 4)],
                          ),
                          child: Text(
                            simQ.question,
                            maxLines: 2,
                            overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                            style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate50, fontSize: 9, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    );
                  }),
                  Align(
                    alignment: Alignment.center,
                    child: Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(color: AppColors.rose500, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4)),
                      child: const Text('當前死穴', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                    ),
                  )
                ],
              );
            },
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

// ==========================================
// 核心刷題視圖狀態機
// ==========================================

class ElectroQuizArenaView extends StatefulWidget {
  const ElectroQuizArenaView({super.key});

  @override
  State<ElectroQuizArenaView> createState() => _ElectroQuizArenaViewState();
}

class _ElectroQuizArenaViewState extends State<ElectroQuizArenaView> {
  String _selectedCategory = 'ALL';
  bool _onlyWrongQueue = false;
  bool _onlyMultipleChoice = false;

  int _currentFilteredIndex = 0;
  List<int> _userSelectedOptions = [];
  bool _isAnswerRevealed = false;
  bool _isSocraticMaskActive = true;

  // Mark 時間戳攔截
  int _qStartTime = DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch;
  int? _firstTapTime;
  int? _lastSwitchTime;

  int _readingSec = 0;
  int _hesitationSec = 0;
  int _decisionSec = 0;

  // Jay Ferro 鎖定盾
  bool _isRatingLocked = false;

  List<QuestionItem> get _filteredQuestions {
    int mask = CarmackBitFlags.matchAll;
    if (_selectedCategory == '00700_B') mask |= CarmackBitFlags.category00700;
    else if (_selectedCategory == '21000_B') mask |= CarmackBitFlags.category21000;
    if (_onlyMultipleChoice) mask |= CarmackBitFlags.typeMultiple;
    return globalMasterState.queryQuestionsFast(queryMask: mask, onlyWrong: _onlyWrongQueue);
  }

  void _resetQuizState() {
    _currentFilteredIndex = 0;
    _userSelectedOptions = [];
    _isAnswerRevealed = false;
    _isSocraticMaskActive = true;
    _isRatingLocked = false;
    _qStartTime = DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch;
    _firstTapTime = null;
    _lastSwitchTime = null;
    _readingSec = 0;
    _hesitationSec = 0;
    _decisionSec = 0;
  }

  void _toggleOption(int index) {
    if (_isAnswerRevealed) return;
    HapticFeedback.lightImpact();
    final now = DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch;

    if (_firstTapTime == null) {
      _firstTapTime = now;
    } else {
      _lastSwitchTime = now;
    }

    final q = _filteredQuestions[_currentFilteredIndex];
    setState(() {
      if (q.type == 'single') {
        _userSelectedOptions = [index + 1];
      } else {
        final optionNumber = index + 1;
        if (_userSelectedOptions.contains(optionNumber)) {
          _userSelectedOptions.remove(optionNumber);
        } else {
          _userSelectedOptions.add(optionNumber);
        }
      }
    });
  }

  void _submitAndGrade() {
    if (_userSelectedOptions.isEmpty) return;
    HapticFeedback.mediumImpact();

    final now = DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch;
    final firstTap = _firstTapTime ?? now;
    final lastSwitch = _lastSwitchTime ?? firstTap;

    _readingSec = math.min(60, math.max(1, ((firstTap - _qStartTime) / 1000).round()));
    _hesitationSec = math.min(60, math.max(0, ((lastSwitch - firstTap) / 1000).round()));
    _decisionSec = math.min(60, math.max(1, ((now - lastSwitch) / 1000).round()));

    setState(() {
      _isAnswerRevealed = true;
      _isSocraticMaskActive = true;
    });
  }

  void _handleRatingSelected(StudyRating rating) {
    if (_isRatingLocked) return;
    setState(() => _isRatingLocked = true);

    final q = _filteredQuestions[_currentFilteredIndex];
    switch (rating) {
      case StudyRating.again: HapticFeedback.heavyImpact(); break;
      case StudyRating.hard: HapticFeedback.mediumImpact(); break;
      case StudyRating.good: HapticFeedback.lightImpact(); break;
      case StudyRating.easy: HapticFeedback.selectionClick(); break;
    }
    
    globalMasterState.processQuestionReview(questionId: q.id, rating: rating);
    
    setState(() {
      _resetQuizState();
      if (_currentFilteredIndex < _filteredQuestions.length - 1) {
        _currentFilteredIndex++;
      } else {
        _currentFilteredIndex = 0;
      }
    });

    Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 400), () {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isRatingLocked = false);
    });
  }

  void _jumpToQuestionById(String targetId) {
    HapticFeedback.selectionClick();
    int idx = _filteredQuestions.indexWhere((item) => item.id == targetId);
    if (idx != -1) {
      setState(() {
        _currentFilteredIndex = idx;
        _resetQuizState();
      });
    }
  }

  String _generateClinicalDiagnosis(QuestionItem q) {
    final text = q.question;
    if (text.contains('電壓降') || text.contains('線路')) return '可能忽略了「單相/三相」的係數差異（如忘記乘上 √3 或 2），導致壓降公式套用錯誤。';
    if (text.contains('距離') || text.contains('毫米') || text.contains('公尺')) return '這是舊版法規或不同情境的數值，請留意題幹的「極限條件（以上/以下）」。';
    if (text.contains('變壓器') || text.contains('V-V')) return '此為容量未乘上 √3 或效率未折減的典型數值誘答。';
    if (text.contains('漏電') || text.contains('接地')) return '請釐清「系統接地」與「設備接地」的法規區別，此選項張冠李戴。';
    return '此為常見的直覺陷阱，未考慮底層物理條件，大腦容易受表面數字誤導。';
  }

  Widget _buildChunkyOptionTile(int index, String text, QuestionItem q) {
    final optionNumber = index + 1;
    final isSelected = _userSelectedOptions.contains(optionNumber);
    final isCorrectOption = q.answer.contains(optionNumber);

    Color borderColor = AppColors.slate800; 
    Color bgColor = AppColors.slate900; 
    Color shadowColor = Colors.black;
    Widget? trailingIcon;
    double pushOffset = 0.0;
    bool showDiagnosis = false;

    if (_isAnswerRevealed) {
      if (isCorrectOption && isSelected) {
        borderColor = AppColors.emerald400; bgColor = AppColors.emerald400.withOpacity(0.15); shadowColor = AppColors.emerald400.withOpacity(0.5); trailingIcon = const Icon(Icons.check_circle, color: AppColors.emerald400); pushOffset = 4.0;
      } else if (isCorrectOption && !isSelected) {
        borderColor = AppColors.amber400; trailingIcon = const Icon(Icons.warning_amber_rounded, color: AppColors.amber400);
      } else if (!isCorrectOption && isSelected) {
        borderColor = AppColors.rose500; bgColor = AppColors.rose500.withOpacity(0.1); shadowColor = AppColors.rose500.withOpacity(0.5); trailingIcon = const Icon(Icons.cancel, color: AppColors.rose500); pushOffset = 4.0;
        showDiagnosis = true;
      }
    } else {
      if (isSelected) { 
        borderColor = AppColors.amber400; bgColor = AppColors.amber400.withOpacity(0.1); shadowColor = AppColors.amber400.withOpacity(0.5); pushOffset = 4.0; 
      }
    }

    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: () => _toggleOption(index),
      child: AnimatedContainer(
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 150),
        curve: Curves.easeOut,
        margin: EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12.0 - pushOffset, top: pushOffset),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: bgColor, 
          border: Border.all(color: borderColor, width: 2), 
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          boxShadow: [ BoxShadow(color: shadowColor, offset: Offset(0, 4.0 - pushOffset)) ],
        ),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Row(
              children: [
                Container(
                  width: 32, height: 32, alignment: Alignment.center,
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(shape: BoxShape.circle, color: isSelected ? AppColors.amber400 : AppColors.slate800),
                  child: Text('($optionNumber)', style: TextStyle(color: isSelected ? AppColors.slate950 : AppColors.slate400, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 13)),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 16),
                Expanded(child: Text(text, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate50, fontSize: 16, height: 1.4))),
                if (trailingIcon != null) ...[const SizedBox(width: 12), trailingIcon],
              ],
            ),
            if (showDiagnosis)
              _ClinicalDiagnosticTag(diagnosticMessage: _generateClinicalDiagnosis(q)),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  // --- 解析與輔助自習 UI ---

  Widget _buildPremiumExplanationCard(QuestionItem q) {
    final exp = q.explanation;
    final isCalculation = q.question.contains('多少') || q.question.contains('計算') || exp.formula.contains('=') || exp.formula.contains('√');
    final similarQuestions = globalMasterState.getRelatedQuestions(q, topK: 3);

    final memoryState = globalMasterState.getCardState(q.id);
    final stability = memoryState.stability;

    bool isAllCorrect = false;
    if (q.type == 'single') {
      isAllCorrect = _userSelectedOptions.isNotEmpty && q.answer.contains(_userSelectedOptions.first);
    } else {
      final uSet = _userSelectedOptions.toSet();
      final aSet = q.answer.toSet();
      isAllCorrect = (uSet.length == aSet.length && uSet.containsAll(aSet));
    }

    if (!isAllCorrect && _isSocraticMaskActive) {
      return _SocraticMaskOverlay(
        questionText: q.question,
        onShatter: () => setState(() => _isSocraticMaskActive = false),
      );
    }

    Widget compactView = Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(color: AppColors.amber400.withOpacity(0.15), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8), border: Border.all(color: AppColors.amber400.withOpacity(0.4))),
      child: Text(exp.quickHack.isNotEmpty ? exp.quickHack : '⚡ 官方速記標準答案，記憶穩定！', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.amber400, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontSize: 15)),
    );

    Widget fullView = Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
      children: [
        if (exp.quickHack.isNotEmpty) ...[
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(color: AppColors.amber400.withOpacity(0.15), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8), border: Border.all(color: AppColors.amber400.withOpacity(0.4))),
            child: Text(exp.quickHack, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.amber400, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontSize: 15)),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
        ],
        if (exp.formula.isNotEmpty) ...[
          Text('📐 核心算式/條號: ${exp.formula}', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate50, fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),

          if (isCalculation)
            _AstTopologyGraph(formula: exp.formula),

          const SizedBox(height: 8),
        ],
        if (exp.stepByStep.isNotEmpty) ...[
          Text('📝 推導: ${exp.stepByStep}', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate300, fontSize: 14, height: 1.5)),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
        ],
        if (exp.fieldNote.isNotEmpty) ...[
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(color: AppColors.slate800.withOpacity(0.5), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6), border: const Border(left: BorderSide(color: AppColors.blue500, width: 4))),
            child: Text('🛠️ 老手筆記: ${exp.fieldNote}', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400, fontSize: 13, height: 1.4)),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
        ],
      ],
    );

    return _AerospaceGroundingScan(
      child: _GlassCard(
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
              children: [
                Row(
                  children: const [
                    Icon(Icons.auto_awesome_rounded, color: AppColors.emerald400, size: 20),
                    SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Text('官方精準解析', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: AppColors.emerald400)),
                  ],
                ),
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 2),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(color: AppColors.emerald400.withOpacity(0.15), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4), border: Border.all(color: AppColors.emerald400.withOpacity(0.5))),
                  child: const Text('BOEING-POST: 100% PASS', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.emerald400, fontSize: 9, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontFamily: 'monospace')),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),

            _MicroDecisionGantt(
              readingSeconds: _readingSec,
              hesitationSeconds: _hesitationSec,
              decisionSeconds: _decisionSec,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),

            _SynapseReplayIndicator(
              currentStability: stability,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),

            _AdaptiveExplanationFolder(
              memoryStability: stability,
              compactChild: compactView,
              fullChild: fullView,
            ),

            const SizedBox(height: 16),

            Row(
              children: [
                Expanded(
                  child: ElevatedButton.icon(
                    onPressed: () {
                      HapticFeedback.mediumImpact();
                      showModalBottomSheet(context: context, isScrollControlled: true, backgroundColor: Colors.transparent, builder: (context) => _AiTutorStreamingSheet(question: q));
                    },
                    icon: const Icon(Icons.psychology_rounded, color: AppColors.slate950, size: 18),
                    label: const Text('AI 大師解剖', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate950, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                    style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: AppColors.emerald400),
                  ),
                ),
                if (isCalculation) ...[
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  Expanded(
                    child: OutlinedButton.icon(
                      onPressed: () {
                        HapticFeedback.mediumImpact();
                        showModalBottomSheet(context: context, isScrollControlled: true, backgroundColor: Colors.transparent, builder: (context) => _AstMathPlaygroundSheet(question: q));
                      },

  }

  Widget _buildFilterBar() {

  @override

class _AiTutorStreamingSheet extends StatefulWidget {
  final QuestionItem question;
  const _AiTutorStreamingSheet({required this.question});
  @override
  State<_AiTutorStreamingSheet> createState() => _AiTutorStreamingSheetState();
}

class _AiTutorStreamingSheetState extends State<_AiTutorStreamingSheet> {
  String _streamedContent = '';
  StreamSubscription<String>? _streamSub;
  final ScrollController _scrollController = ScrollController();
  bool _isGenerating = true;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _startReasoningStream();
  }

  void _startReasoningStream() {
    _streamSub = AiTutorService.streamReasoningChain(question: widget.question).listen(
      (token) {
        if (mounted) {
          setState(() => _streamedContent += token);
          if (_scrollController.hasClients) _scrollController.jumpTo(_scrollController.position.maxScrollExtent);
        }
      },
      onDone: () { if (mounted) { setState(() => _isGenerating = false); HapticFeedback.lightImpact(); } },
      onError: (err) { if (mounted) setState(() { _streamedContent += "\n❌ 串流中斷：$err"; _isGenerating = false; }); },
    );
  }

  @override
  void dispose() { _streamSub?.cancel(); _scrollController.dispose(); super.dispose(); }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return _GlassCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          Center(child: Container(width: 40, height: 4, decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.white30, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2)))),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Row(children: const [Icon(Icons.psychology_rounded, color: AppColors.emerald400, size: 22), SizedBox(width: 8), Text('AI 大師解剖', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: AppColors.slate50))]),
              if (!_isGenerating) const Icon(Icons.verified_user_rounded, color: AppColors.emerald400, size: 18)
              else const SizedBox(width: 16, height: 16, child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: AppColors.amber400, strokeWidth: 2)),
            ],
          ),
          const Divider(color: Colors.white24, height: 24),
          Expanded(child: SingleChildScrollView(controller: _scrollController, child: Text(_streamedContent.isEmpty ? "⚡ 構建推導中..." : _streamedContent, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate50, fontSize: 14, height: 1.6, fontFamily: 'monospace')))),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          _DuoChunkyButton(text: _isGenerating ? '停止並返回' : '領悟完成，關閉', color: AppColors.slate700, shadowColor: Colors.black, height: 48, onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _AstMathPlaygroundSheet extends StatefulWidget {
  final QuestionItem question;
  const _AstMathPlaygroundSheet({required this.question});
  @override
  State<_AstMathPlaygroundSheet> createState() => _AstMathPlaygroundSheetState();
}

class _AstMathPlaygroundSheetState extends State<_AstMathPlaygroundSheet> {
  late String _formulaText;
  double _paramA = 50.0;
  String? _liveResult;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _deduceFormula();
    _recalculate();
  }

  void _deduceFormula() {
    final q = widget.question.question;
    if (q.contains('V-V')) { _formulaText = 'sqrt3 * P1'; _paramA = 50.0; } 
    else if (q.contains('rpm')) { _formulaText = '(120 * f) / 4'; _paramA = 60.0; } 
    else if (q.contains('焦耳')) { _formulaText = '0.5 * 0.0001 * (V ^ 2)'; _paramA = 100.0; } 
    else { _formulaText = 'sqrt3 * V * 50 * 0.85'; _paramA = 220.0; }
  }

  void _recalculate() {
    try {
      final res = ElectricalAstEvaluator.evaluate(_formulaText, {'P1': _paramA, 'f': _paramA, 'V': _paramA});
      setState(() => _liveResult = res.toStringAsFixed(2));
    } catch (e) {
      setState(() => _liveResult = '語法異常');
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return _GlassCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
      child: Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: const [
              Row(children: [Icon(Icons.science_rounded, color: AppColors.amber400), SizedBox(width: 8), Text('AST 動態沙盒', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: AppColors.slate50))]),
            ],
          ),
          const Divider(color: Colors.white24, height: 24),
          Container(padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12), decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.black45, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)), child: Text(_formulaText, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: AppColors.amber400, fontFamily: 'monospace'))),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          Text('滑動調整核心電氣參數 (${_paramA.round()}):', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate50, fontSize: 13)),
          Slider(value: _paramA, min: 10.0, max: 440.0, divisions: 43, activeColor: AppColors.amber400, inactiveColor: AppColors.slate700, onChanged: (val) { setState(() { _paramA = val; _recalculate(); }); }),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16), decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.black45, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8), border: Border.all(color: AppColors.emerald400)),
            child: Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween, children: [const Text('運算結果：', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400, fontSize: 13)), Text(_liveResult ?? '計算中...', style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 24, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: AppColors.emerald400, fontFamily: 'monospace'))]),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          _DuoChunkyButton(text: '返回刷題', color: AppColors.slate700, shadowColor: Colors.black, height: 48, onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// 專案名稱：電光證照霸 PRO (ElectroSolar Pass)

// CFO + CIO 注入：2026 Google Gemini 最新官方計價矩陣與智慧模型路由

import 'dart:async';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import '../models/core_types.dart';
import '../utils/telemetry_logger.dart';
import '../utils/regulation_grounding_engine.dart';

/// FedEx 級解題物流站點狀態
enum LogisticsPhase {
  extract('📦', '提取考點參數'),
  formula('🚚', '裝載底層公式'),
  trap('🛑', '排雷與防呆掃描'),
  delivery('✅', '最終結論交付');

  final String icon;
  final String label;
  const LogisticsPhase(this.icon, this.label);
}

/// 封裝單個解析區塊的物流包裹
class LogisticsNode {
  final LogisticsPhase phase;
  final List<String> lines;
  LogisticsNode(this.phase, this.lines);
}

/// 2026 年 10 月 Google Gemini 官方模型定價階層
enum GeminiModelTier {
  localZeroCost(
    id: 'local-prebaked-argon-distilled',
    name: '本地固化引擎 (Argon 預製蒸餾 + AST)',
    inputPerMillionUSD: 0.0,
    outputPerMillionUSD: 0.0,
    cachePerMillionUSD: 0.0,
    maxThinkingBudget: 0,
  ),
  flashLite25(
    id: 'gemini-2.5-flash-lite',
    name: 'Gemini 2.5 Flash-Lite (超輕量速答)',
    inputPerMillionUSD: 0.10,
    outputPerMillionUSD: 0.40,
    cachePerMillionUSD: 0.01,
    maxThinkingBudget: 128,
  ),
  flashLite35(
    id: 'gemini-3.5-flash-lite',
    name: 'Gemini 3.5 Flash-Lite (高頻代理主力)',
    inputPerMillionUSD: 0.30,
    outputPerMillionUSD: 2.50,
    cachePerMillionUSD: 0.03,
    maxThinkingBudget: 256,
  ),
  flash38(
    id: 'gemini-3.8-flash',
    name: 'Gemini 3.8 Flash (工程推導主力)',
    // 2026/12/31 前優惠價 $0.75 / $3.75 (2027/1/1 起翻倍為 $1.50 / $7.50)
    inputPerMillionUSD: 0.75,
    outputPerMillionUSD: 3.75,
    cachePerMillionUSD: 0.075,
    maxThinkingBudget: 512,
  ),
  argon4(
    id: 'gemini-4-argon',
    name: 'Gemini 4 Argon (旗艦深層推理)',
    // 2026/11/30 前優惠價 $2.00 / $10.00 (2026/12/1 起翻倍為 $4.00 / $20.00)
    inputPerMillionUSD: 2.00,
    outputPerMillionUSD: 10.00,
    cachePerMillionUSD: 0.10,
    maxThinkingBudget: 1024,
  );

  final String id;
  final String name;
  final double inputPerMillionUSD;
  final double outputPerMillionUSD;
  final double cachePerMillionUSD;
  final int maxThinkingBudget;

  const GeminiModelTier({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.inputPerMillionUSD,
    required this.outputPerMillionUSD,
    required this.cachePerMillionUSD,
    required this.maxThinkingBudget,
  });
}

class AiTutorService {
  static const String modelPro = "gemini-3.8-flash";
  static const String modelLite = "gemini-3.5-flash-lite";

  /// 【CFO 智慧路由器】：根據問題複雜度與是否為動態追問，自動派發最具成本效益的模型
  static GeminiModelTier resolveOptimalTier({
    required QuestionItem question,
    String? customStudentDoubt,
  }) {
    // 1. 若無額外自由提問，100% 走本地已固化的 Argon 級預製詳解 ($0 邊際成本)
    if (customStudentDoubt == null || customStudentDoubt.trim().isEmpty) {
      return GeminiModelTier.localZeroCost;
    }

    // 2. 若有自由提問，判斷是否為高難度複合計算題
    final doubt = customStudentDoubt.trim();
    final isComplexMath = doubt.contains('推導') ||
        doubt.contains('為什麼算出來') ||
        doubt.contains('公式') ||
        question.difficulty == 3;

    if (isComplexMath) {
      // 複雜工程計算：派發給 Gemini 3.8 Flash (具備高精度推理且受 512 Thinking Budget 保護)
      return GeminiModelTier.flash38;
    }

    // 3. 一般法規與觀念追問：派發給 Gemini 3.5 Flash-Lite
    return GeminiModelTier.flashLite35;
  }

  /// 【FedEx 級串流調度器】：產生四階結構化電工思維鏈
  static Stream<String> streamReasoningChain({
    required QuestionItem question,
    String? customStudentDoubt,
  }) async* {
    // 1. 透過 CFO 智慧路由器決定模型層級
    final selectedTier = resolveOptimalTier(
      question: question,
      customStudentDoubt: customStudentDoubt,
    );

    // 2. 取得結構化物流節點
    final nodes = _synthesizeLogisticsNodes(question, customStudentDoubt, selectedTier);

    // 3. 組合全文並過法規真理安檢閘門 (Grounding Engine - 省下 $14/1000次 Search 規費)
    final fullText = nodes.expand((n) => n.lines).join(" ");
    final audit = RegulationGroundingEngine.auditContent(fullText);

    // 4. 依 2026 官方新表精算 Token 與 Thinking 財務成本
    final visibleOutputTokens = (fullText.length / 1.5).round();
    final estimatedInputTokens = (question.question.length + question.options.join().length + (customStudentDoubt?.length ?? 0)) ~/ 1.2 + 300;
    
    final costReport = estimateInferenceCost2026(
      inputTokens: estimatedInputTokens,
      visibleOutputTokens: visibleOutputTokens,
      tier: selectedTier,
      useContextCache: true,
    );

    telemetry.logEvent(
      eventName: 'AI_CFO_COST_AUDIT',
      parameters: {
        'questionId': question.id,
        'routedModel': selectedTier.id,
        'thinkingBudget': selectedTier.maxThinkingBudget,
        'costUSD': costReport['costUSD'],
        'costNTD': costReport['costNTD'],
        'isFactGrounded': audit.isSafe,
      },
    );

    if (kDebugMode) {
      debugPrint('💰 [CFO AI 成本審計] 路由模型: ${selectedTier.id} | 預估耗費: NT\$ ${costReport['costNTD']} 元 (含 Thinking Budget: ${selectedTier.maxThinkingBudget})');
    }

    // 5. 依物流站點分塊送達 (Chunked Delivery)
    for (int i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) {
      final node = nodes[i];
      final header = "\n\n${node.phase.icon} [站點 ${i + 1}/${nodes.length}] ${node.phase.label}\n";
      for (int j = 0; j < header.length; j++) {
        yield header[j];
        await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 12));
      }

      for (final line in node.lines) {
        final formattedLine = "  ‣ $line\n";
        for (int k = 0; k < formattedLine.length; k++) {
          yield formattedLine[k];
          await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 6));
        }
        await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 250));
      }
    }

    // 6. 附加法規校準與路由認證標記
    if (!audit.isSafe) {
      final groundingStr = "\n\n🛡️ [系統最高攔截] 偵測到數值偏移，已由本地法規庫強制覆寫為國家標準：\n  ‣ ${audit.calibratedNote}\n";
      for (int i = 0; i < groundingStr.length; i++) {
        yield groundingStr[i];
        await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 8));
      }
    } else {
      final safeStr = "\n\n🛡️ [法規安檢] 100% 通過國家標準檢驗 (路由引擎: ${selectedTier.name})。\n";
      for (int i = 0; i < safeStr.length; i++) {
        yield safeStr[i];
        await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 4));
      }
    }
  }

  /// 【2026 官方價目表精算器】：納入 Thinking Tokens 與 Context Caching 折扣
  static Map<String, dynamic> estimateInferenceCost2026({
    required int inputTokens,
    required int visibleOutputTokens,
    required GeminiModelTier tier,
    bool useContextCache = true,
  }) {
    if (tier == GeminiModelTier.localZeroCost) {
      return {
        'model': tier.id,
        'totalInputTokens': inputTokens,
        'totalOutputTokens': visibleOutputTokens,
        'costUSD': '0.000000',
        'costNTD': '0.0000',
      };
    }

    // 若啟用 Context Caching，80% 的系統與法規背景 Prompt 適用 Cache 1 折價
    final int cachedInput = useContextCache ? (inputTokens * 0.8).round() : 0;
    final int uncachedInput = inputTokens - cachedInput;

    // 輸出 Token = 實際可見文字 + 受控的 Thinking Tokens
    final int totalOutput = visibleOutputTokens + tier.maxThinkingBudget;

    // 判斷是否已跨越 2026/12/1 或 2027/1/1 漲價線
    final now = DateTime.now();
    double priceMultiplier = 1.0;
    if (tier == GeminiModelTier.argon4 && now.isAfter(DateTime(2026, 11, 30))) {
      priceMultiplier = 2.0; // 12/1 起翻倍
    } else if (tier == GeminiModelTier.flash38 && now.isAfter(DateTime(2026, 12, 31))) {
      priceMultiplier = 2.0; // 2027/1/1 起翻倍
    }

    final double inputCost = ((uncachedInput / 1000000.0) * tier.inputPerMillionUSD +
            (cachedInput / 1000000.0) * tier.cachePerMillionUSD) *
        priceMultiplier;
    final double outputCost = (totalOutput / 1000000.0) * tier.outputPerMillionUSD * priceMultiplier;

    final double totalUSD = inputCost + outputCost;
    final double totalNTD = totalUSD * 32.5;

    return {
      'model': tier.id,
      'totalInputTokens': inputTokens,
      'totalOutputTokens': totalOutput,
      'costUSD': totalUSD.toStringAsFixed(6),
      'costNTD': totalNTD.toStringAsFixed(4),
    };
  }

  /// 向下相容舊版呼叫介面
  static Map<String, dynamic> estimateInferenceCost({
    required int inputTokens,
    required int outputTokens,
    String model = modelPro,
  }) {
    final tier = model == modelLite ? GeminiModelTier.flashLite35 : GeminiModelTier.flash38;
    return estimateInferenceCost2026(
      inputTokens: inputTokens,
      visibleOutputTokens: outputTokens,
      tier: tier,
    );
  }

  /// 【FedEx 核心打包廠】：將解析資料封裝進 4 大物流節點
  static List<LogisticsNode> _synthesizeLogisticsNodes(
    QuestionItem q,
    String? doubt,
    GeminiModelTier tier,
  ) {
    final ansStr = q.answer.map((a) => "($a)").join("、");
    final isCalc = q.question.contains('多少') || q.question.contains('計算') || q.question.contains('kVA') || q.question.contains('Ω');
    final isReg = q.question.contains('法規') || q.question.contains('規定') || q.question.contains('公尺') || q.question.contains('毫米');

    final List<LogisticsNode> nodes = [];

    // 站點 1：提取考點參數
    final List<String> extractLines = [];
    if (isCalc) {
      extractLines.add("題型研判：高難度電學數值計算題（已聯動本地 AST 確定性運算核心）。");
      extractLines.add("首要動作：抓取電源相數與線路參數，嚴防單相/三相公式混淆。");
    } else if (isReg) {
      extractLines.add("題型研判：國家電工法規強制標準題（已綁定本地法規防幻覺閘門）。");
      extractLines.add("首要動作：鎖定處所限制與關鍵數字（特別留意以上/以下）。");
    } else {
      extractLines.add("題型研判：電工機械結構與保護協調原理題。");
      extractLines.add("首要動作：回歸電磁感應本質分析動作邏輯。");
    }
    nodes.add(LogisticsNode(LogisticsPhase.extract, extractLines));

    // 站點 2：裝載底層公式
    final List<String> formulaLines = [];
    if (q.explanation.formula.isNotEmpty) {
      formulaLines.add("核心公式/法規依據：${q.explanation.formula}");
    }
    if (q.explanation.stepByStep.isNotEmpty) {
      final steps = q.explanation.stepByStep.split('\n').where((s) => s.trim().isNotEmpty);
      formulaLines.addAll(steps);
    } else {
      formulaLines.add("經比對，官方公告標準答案為選項 $ansStr。");
    }
    nodes.add(LogisticsNode(LogisticsPhase.formula, formulaLines));

    // 站點 3：排雷與防呆掃描
    final List<String> trapLines = [];
    if (q.type == 'multiple') {
      trapLines.add("⚠️ 複選題警戒：每個選項皆為獨立是非題，全對才給分，切勿猜題！");
    }
    trapLines.add("秒殺口訣：${q.explanation.quickHack.isNotEmpty ? q.explanation.quickHack : '熟讀法規條件，建立直覺反射'}");
    nodes.add(LogisticsNode(LogisticsPhase.trap, trapLines));

    // 站點 4：最終結論交付
    final List<String> deliveryLines = [];
    if (q.explanation.fieldNote.isNotEmpty) {
      deliveryLines.add("實務筆記：${q.explanation.fieldNote}");
    } else {
      deliveryLines.add("工安宣導：現場施工落實停電、驗電、掛接地線，工安無妥協空間。");
    }

    if (doubt != null && doubt.trim().isNotEmpty) {
      deliveryLines.add("針對您的專屬提問：「$doubt」");
      deliveryLines.add("大師點評 (${tier.id})：請鎖定核心物理量守恆與 AST 沙盒滑桿驗證，換個數字也能秒殺！");
    }

    nodes.add(LogisticsNode(LogisticsPhase.delivery, deliveryLines));
    return nodes;
  }
}
